Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the poincare framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 582. The resulting operator achieves bound O(807/n^5).

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to poincare structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 582/807 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.
